# gcp-other adversarial verification

Input requirements: 1061; corrections: 75.
Each requirement is audited below. Evidence paths come from the indexed evaluator closure source; proposed checks remain unimplemented.

## Changes

### ccc_gcp / CCC.Build.CN01.AR01

- NEW_CHECK [gcp_cloudbuild_trigger_trust] → PARTIAL [gcp_cloudbuild_trigger_trust]
- Why: Build trigger or worker-pool configuration gives a read-only trust-boundary signal. The specified unauthorized build or external access attempt being rejected requires a runtime test.

### ccc_gcp / CCC.Build.CN02.AR01

- NEW_CHECK [gcp_cloudbuild_trigger_trust] → PARTIAL [gcp_cloudbuild_trigger_trust]
- Why: Build trigger or worker-pool configuration gives a read-only trust-boundary signal. The specified unauthorized build or external access attempt being rejected requires a runtime test.

### ccc_gcp / CCC.Build.CN03.AR01

- NEW_CHECK [gcp_cloudbuild_private_pool] → PARTIAL [gcp_cloudbuild_private_pool]
- Why: Build trigger or worker-pool configuration gives a read-only trust-boundary signal. The specified unauthorized build or external access attempt being rejected requires a runtime test.

### ccc_gcp / CCC.Core.CN01.AR01

- PARTIAL [gcp.ports.reference_sql_ssl_connections, gcp_compute_ssl_tls13] → PARTIAL [gcp.ports.reference_sql_ssl_connections]
- Why: Cloud SQL sslMode/requireSsl is a direct TLS requirement signal for SQL only. the strict-declaration evaluator treats TLS_1_3 as unrecognized (peaso/checks/gcp_ports_relationships.py:65-70), and a configuration read cannot prove every non-SSH port negotiated TLS 1.3.

### ccc_gcp / CCC.Core.CN06.AR01

- NEW_CHECK [gcp_resource_approved_location] → PARTIAL [gcp_resource_approved_location]
- Why: Cloud Asset Inventory location plus an approved-location policy is a read-only residency signal. It cannot establish availability-zone placement for every child resource or all data replication destinations and their trust perimeter.

### ccc_gcp / CCC.Core.CN06.AR02

- NEW_CHECK [gcp_resource_approved_location] → PARTIAL [gcp_resource_approved_location]
- Why: Cloud Asset Inventory location plus an approved-location policy is a read-only residency signal. It cannot establish availability-zone placement for every child resource or all data replication destinations and their trust perimeter.

### ccc_gcp / CCC.Core.CN07.AR02

- NEW_CHECK [gcp_logging_enumeration_alert] → PARTIAL [gcp.claude.project.audit_logs_configured]
- Why: Audit logging configuration is a partial signal. An enumeration alert configuration does not prove each event was logged with client identity, time and activity; inspect representative audit entries.

### ccc_gcp / CCC.Core.CN08.AR02

- MANUAL [none] → PARTIAL [gcp_gcs_replication_status]
- Why: Cloud Storage bucket metadata can expose a declared dual-region replica topology. Replication synchronization status for all services needs additional service telemetry or operational evidence.

- NEW_CHECK [gcp_certificate_validity] → PARTIAL [gcp_certificate_validity]

## Spot checks

- `peaso/checks/gcp_ports_relationships.py:65` treats TLS_1_3 as unrecognized while `peaso/checks/claude_gcp_k8s_compute.py:587` accepts it as a version; neither evaluates a TLS 1.3-only policy, so this remains partial.
- `peaso/checks/gcp_ports_perimeter.py:94` checks broad authorizedNetworks, while `peaso/checks/gcp.py:11` checks SQL public IPv4.
- `peaso/checks/gcp_ports_iam.py:39` has distinct KMS and service-account separation predicates; the earlier Prowler service-account mapping used the KMS one.
- `peaso/checks/claude_gcp_k8s_iam.py:743` rejects issued GKE client certificates, contrary to the literal source control.
- `peaso/checks/gcp_ports_services.py:220` requires SQL Server trace flag 3625 on; a source control requests off.
- `peaso/collectors/gcp.py:1226,1334,1450,1521` collect API keys, Pub/Sub topics, Cloud Build triggers, and Workbench instances respectively.

## Requirement audit

- `CCC.Core.CN01.AR01` PARTIAL: gcp.ports.reference_sql_ssl_connections; evaluator peaso/checks/gcp_ports_perimeter.py:108. ## Remaining doubts

- Some broad organizational controls retain PARTIAL mappings that represent only one cloud configuration signal; the ledger reason must not be read as proof of the whole control.
- Proposed checks using Certificate Manager, Security Command Center, OS Config, and Cloud Asset Inventory require new read-only collection and coverage handling.
- The source itself contains controls that request legacy or anti-hardening settings (GKE client certificates, SQL Server 3625 off); these are represented literally and flagged above.


## Additional adversarial corrections

- `gcp-compliance.benchmark.nist_csf_v2 / gcp-compliance.control.compute_target_https_uses_latest_tls_version`: PARTIAL [gcp.claude.target_proxy.https_min_tls_1_2, gcp_compute_ssl_tls13] → PARTIAL [gcp.ports.compute_ssl_policy_strict_declaration]. The existing HTTPS-proxy evaluator inspects SSL policy profile, minimum TLS declaration and weak ciphers. It accepts a minimum only through TLS_1_2; the prior TLS_1_3 minimum proposal is not implementable with this field and actual TLS negotiation requires an endpoint probe.
- `ccc_gcp / CCC.MLDE.CN07.AR02`: PARTIAL [gcp_workbench_no_public_ip] → MANUAL [none]. The approved-public-access requirement is about compensating controls and authorization. disablePublicIp does not test those conditions for a nonsensitive instance.
- `ens_rd2022_gcp / op.mon.3.r1.gcp.scc.1`: NEW_CHECK [gcp_logging_scc_findings_alert] → MANUAL [none]. An alert route for high-severity SCC findings does not correlate events or perform dynamic analysis. Evidence of the monitoring procedure and analyzed cases is required.
- `ens_rd2022_gcp / op.mon.3.r2.gcp.scc.1`: NEW_CHECK [gcp_logging_scc_findings_alert] → MANUAL [none]. An alert route for high-severity SCC findings does not correlate events or perform dynamic analysis. Evidence of the monitoring procedure and analyzed cases is required.
- `ens_rd2022_gcp / op.exp.6.r2.gcp.scc.1`: PARTIAL [gcp.ports.project_service_cloudasset_api_enabled] → MANUAL [none]. Cloud Asset Inventory API enablement does not perform the requested preventive system review; review execution records are required.
- `mitre_attack_gcp / T1190`: PARTIAL [gcp.claude.sql_instance.second_gen_no_public_ip, gcp.compute.no_external_ip, gcp.ports.compute_firewall_rule_rdp_access_restricted, gcp.ports.compute_firewall_rule_ssh_access_restricted, gcp.ports.sql_instance_not_open_to_internet, gcp.reviewed.kms.no_public_iam, gcp.storage.no_public_iam] → MANUAL [none]. The earlier native configuration checks are mitigations or unrelated posture snapshots; they do not detect the adversary activity described by this ATT&CK technique. Event or host telemetry and analyst correlation are required.

## Proposed predicate repairs

- `gcp_cloud_armor_rate_limit`: narrowed the source/predicate to the actual resource fields and requirement threshold. FAIL when an internet-facing backend lacks a matching throttle or rate-based ban rule with count at most 2000 per intervalSec at most 300 and banDurationSec at least 60; UNKNOWN when backend association, rule precedence, or per-client key is unavailable.
- `gcp_compute_approved_image`: narrowed the source/predicate to the actual resource fields and requirement threshold. FAIL when a VM boot disk sourceImage is outside approved_image_projects; UNKNOWN when the boot disk origin was not retained or the approval list is unavailable.
- `gcp_gcs_replication_status`: narrowed the source/predicate to the actual resource fields and requirement threshold. FAIL when the declared replica locations differ from expected_replica_locations; UNKNOWN when the policy or service synchronization status is unavailable.
- `gcp_cloudcdn_cookie_ttl`: narrowed the source/predicate to the actual resource fields and requirement threshold. FAIL when GENERATED_COOKIE or HTTP_COOKIE affinity has affinityCookieTtlSec above 1800 seconds; UNKNOWN when cookie TTL is absent. Absolute affinity TTL cannot establish expiry after inactivity.

- 1,061 input requirements matched exactly once across 18 frameworks; every native id resolves to the check catalogue or to a same-row new-check specification.

## Repair pass

Reviewed every requirement framework by framework against frozen input text, indexed evaluator scope, and canonical proposed checks. Proposed IDs are recorded only in `new_checks`; they are not native runtime tags.

- Requirements reviewed: 1061; rows changed: 180.
- Decision counts before: {'PARTIAL': 377, 'MANUAL': 228, 'NEW_CHECK': 34, 'NOT_APPLICABLE': 4, 'MAPPED': 408, 'MAPPING_FIX': 10}.
- Decision counts after: {'PARTIAL': 409, 'MANUAL': 228, 'NEW_CHECK': 34, 'NOT_APPLICABLE': 4, 'MAPPED': 376, 'MAPPING_FIX': 10}.
- Scope corrections: audit-policy inheritance, backend-bucket logging, GKE node exclusions, service-account scope, Cloud Functions IAM, and the SQL Server user-connections flag.
- Manual rows with generic rationales now identify the requirement and matching evidence records. Proposed enumeration and query-rate alerts now name fields and thresholds.
- Spot-check corrections for `CCC.Build.CN01.AR01` and SecNumCloud `18.3` were confirmed in the ledger.

Additional review: 20 rows refined. Cloud Identity password-policy and ATT&CK T1201 are manual evidence reviews, not provider exclusions; Workbench download monitoring requires event evidence. ATT&CK event proposals now name exact audit fields and predicates. Final decision counts: {'PARTIAL': 409, 'MANUAL': 231, 'NEW_CHECK': 33, 'NOT_APPLICABLE': 2, 'MAPPED': 376, 'MAPPING_FIX': 10}.
